<script type="text/javascript">
//<![CDATA[
  var resPath = '/_custom/postech/resource/', isEditorImageUpload = 'true' == 'false'? false: true, clientId = 'postech', boardTy = 'regulations', boardNo = '16', siteId = 'kor';

<script type="text/javascript">

function runAddAction( _self, popupEl ){

		var articleNo = _self.data("articleNo");
		var id = _self.data("id");
		var articleTitle = _self.data("title");
		var categoryNm = _self.data("cate");
		var locale = _self.data("locale");

		$.ajax({
			url:'/board/regulations/history.do',
			//traditional: true,	-- 배열 넘기는 속성
			dataType:'json',
			method: 'post',
			data: {
				'articleNo' : articleNo,
				'id' : id
			},
			success: function( json ) {

				var resultList = json.resultList;
				var html = "";

				if( resultList.length > 0 ){

					$("h5.h5-tit01").text('[' + categoryNm + '] ' + articleTitle );

					$(resultList).each(function(index, item){

						html += '<tr>';
						html += '<td>' + (resultList.length - index) + '</td>';
						html += '<td>' + item.regDt + '</td>';
						html += '<td>' + item.regNo + '</td>';
						html += '<td>';
						if( item.regFilePath != null && item.regEncodeNm != null ){

							var ext = item.regEncodeNm && item.regEncodeNm.split('.').pop().toLowerCase();
							if( ext == 'pdf' ){
								html += '<a class="link_btn" href="' + '/app/board/regulations/pdf/reg_' + item.id + '.do' + '" target="_blank"><span>' + (locale == "ko" ? "보기" : "View" ) + '</span></a></td>';
							}else{
								html += '<a class="link_btn" href="' + '?mode=regulationsDownload&type=reg&articleNo=' + item.articleNo + '&id=' + item.id + '"><span class="download">' + (locale == "ko" ? "보기" : "View" ) + '</span></a></td>';
							}

						}
						html += '</td>';

						if( locale == 'ko' ){
							html += '<td>';
							if( item.oldNewFilePath != '' && item.oldNewEncodeNm != '' ){

								var ext = item.oldNewEncodeNm && item.oldNewEncodeNm.split('.').pop().toLowerCase();
								if( ext == 'pdf' ){
									html += '<a class="link_btn" href="' + '/app/board/regulations/pdf/oldNew_' + item.id + '.do' + '" target="_blank"><span>보기</span></a></td>';
								}else{
									html += '<a class="link_btn" href="' + '?mode=regulationsDownload&type=oldNew&articleNo=' + item.articleNo + '&id=' + item.id + '"><span class="download">보기</span></a></td>';
								}

							}
							html += '</td>';
						}

						html += '</tr>';

					});

					$(".popup-table tbody").html('').append(html);

				}else{

					$("h5.h5-tit01").text('[' + categoryNm + '] ' + articleTitle );
					html += '<tr><td colspan="' + (locale == 'ko' ? 5 : 4) + '">' + (locale == 'ko' ? '과거규정 이력이 존재하지 않습니다.' : 'No data') + '</td></tr>';
					$(".popup-table tbody").html('').append(html);
				}

				$(".regulation_history").addClass("open");
				$("body").addClass("popon");
			}
		});


	}

</script>
